\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{dxc4}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{dxc4} (\texttt{d5c4}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0022\#650} (ply 22) [structural distance $d$=79, QAP obj=91]: the master played \texttt{d5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pd4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{N}f6 as their \emph{Q}d1, our \emph{P}e6 as their \emph{B}c2; transferred move \texttt{d5c4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2025-11\_p0000\#2909} (ply 22) [structural distance $d$=79, QAP obj=91]: the master played \texttt{d5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pd4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{N}f6 as their \emph{Q}d1, our \emph{P}e6 as their \emph{B}c2; transferred move \texttt{d5c4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0020\#3751} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=79, QAP obj=65]: the master played \texttt{cxb5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pc4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{P}f5 as their \emph{B}c1, our \emph{N}f6 as their \emph{R}f1; transferred move \texttt{d5c4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 4 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{dxc4} (summed QAP objective 302, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
